Covers §13.1–§13.4, §13.6 and handbook §1. Companion to the other Tor and onion-network skills. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/tor-and-onion-networks/skills/tor-censorship-circumvention-and-bridges) · ★2)
- **tor-ecosystem-alternatives-and-governance**: Use when choosing a Tor implementation or client (C tor vs Arti, Tor Browser, Tor VPN, Orbot, Tails, Whonix), picking operator tooling (nyx, stem, txtorcon, chutney), comparing Tor against I2P, mixnets or VPNs, or looking up the specs, proposals and foundational papers. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/tor-and-onion-networks/skills/tor-ecosystem-alternatives-and-governance) · ★2)
- **tor-network-consensus-guards-and-paths**: Use when reasoning about the Tor network as an operated system — relays and flags, the nine directory authorities, the hourly consensus, bandwidth weights, guard pinning and why it exists, path-selection restrictions, and happy families — or when looking up a relay with Relay Search, Onionoo, ExoneraTor or CollecTor. Covers §6–§7. Companion to the other Tor and onion-network skills.
